We collected 237 step-numbered solutions to 79 Humanity's Last Exam physics questions from three frontier models, with no inserted errors, and independently labelled final-answer correctness and the first false step. The reference standard combined physicist annotations, an independent LLM debate, and source-masked adjudication. This yielded 24 critical traces in which the answer was correct but the trace contained a genuine error. 8 LLM monitors evaluated traces blind, with an unverified or certified answer, or after a blind commitment. Certification raised mean balanced accuracy from 0.637 to 0.796, while exact first-error localization rose from 0.261 to 0.379. Certification changed recall (the fraction of error traces flagged as erroneous) from 0.653 to 0.951 on wrong-answer traces but from 0.521 to 0.438 on critical traces; the contrast had the same direction for all 8 monitors (question-bootstrap 95% CI [+0.256, +0.506]). After blind commitment, monitors shown the answer newly flagged 93.8% of previously passed wrong-answer traces as erroneous, but only 18.0% of critical traces. Answer access therefore improves conclusion-consistency checking rather than independent verification of the supporting argument. For AI safety, these traces provide a benign analogue of reward hacking: an acceptable output does not establish that the process producing it was sound. Although the errors studied here were ordinary and mostly non-load-bearing rather than adversarial, trusted-answer evaluations may similarly overstate monitoring capability when acceptable outputs conceal unsound reasoning.

</details>


### [272] [Autoresearch for Marketplace Catalogs: From Legacy Forms to AI-Native Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00274)
*Kartik Ravisankar,Hojat Abdolanezhad,Daniel Capo,Sang Su Lee,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化研究（autoresearch）循环系统，用于在AI原生的双边服务市场中按职业逐一生成供给侧偏好分类法。该系统自2026年4月起在美国某大型消费服务平台生产环境中部署，覆盖132个职业，通过迭代的提出-评估-保留循环为每个职业独立生成标签集，并使用六项标准LLM评判框架和七批评委面板进行打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边服务市场正从确定性表单驱动的请求输入转向AI原生的概率匹配。依赖LLM从自然语言中推断意图而非固定表单字段，迫使平台重新生成供给侧偏好分类法——即既要对服务提供者可解释、又要对市场化决策有用的属性体系。现有的单一全局层级结构无法满足这一新兴需求。

Method: 提出一个自动化研究循环系统：(1) 将每个职业视为独立的生成问题而非全局层级，运行迭代的提出-评估-保留精化循环；(2) 每个候选标签集由重校准后的六项标准LLM-as-judge框架评分；(3) 一个由7个不同人设批评家组成的小组贡献加权惩罚分数调整最终得分（无硬性否决）；(4) 独立的奇偶性映射阶段将传统表单问答对映射回生成的分类法，其方法先推断每个传统问题旨在衡量的供给侧属性，而非逐字翻译问题到标签。

Result: 系统已在美国某主要消费者服务市场平台的生产环境中部署，自2026年4月起覆盖132个职业。奇偶性映射阶段同时提供覆盖率信号和人工质量保证的接口。

Conclusion: 该工作将分类法生成视为面向LLM时代市场基础设施的可扩展自动研究问题，实现了从固定表单到AI原生意图推断的平稳过渡，同时保留了人工质量监督的途径。

Abstract: Two-sided service marketplaces are moving from deterministic request-form intake to AI-native probabilistic matching, enabled by large language models (LLMs) that infer intent, preferences, and latent constraints from natural language. Relying on inferred intent rather than fixed-form fields forces these platforms to regenerate the provider-side preference taxonomy underwriting matching, search, and pricing: attributes interpretable to service providers while remaining a useful signal for marketplace decisions. We present an autoresearch loop that generates this taxonomy, one occupation at a time, and has been deployed in production at a major U.S. consumer services marketplace since April 2026, spanning 132 occupations. Instead of one global hierarchy, the loop treats each occupation as an independent generation problem and runs iterative propose-evaluate-keep refinement cycles. Each candidate tag set is scored by a recalibrated six-rubric LLM-as-judge framework, and a 7-critic panel of distinct personas contributes weighted penalties to an adjusted score, with no hard vetoes. A separate parity-mapping stage maps legacy request-form Q&A pairs back to the generated taxonomy, yielding both a coverage signal and an interface for human quality assurance; it does so by first inferring the provider attribute each legacy question was meant to measure, rather than translating questions to tags literally.

</details>


### [273] [The Irreversibility Budget: Fleet-Level Risk Accounting and Admission Control for Agent Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00275)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可逆性预算"（irreversibility budget），作为一种在可信运行时中跨代理、工作流和租户维护的累积风险价值账户，用于防止LLM代理舰队在共享触发条件下通过逐个通过本地检查而集体超支主体的风险限额。研究显示，逐效果门控可允许舰队级超支高达租户风险限额的48倍，而预算机制能将每个正确计费的运行都控制在限额内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理舰队正在外部化无法完全撤销的影响（移动资金、部署代码、删除数据、披露信息），而当前的控制手段一次只检查一个效果。这使得一支由各自都被单独授权的代理组成的舰队，可以在每个本地门控都保持正确的情况下，在共享触发条件下超支主体的风险——形成"合成授权"层面的系统性风险失控。

Method: 将不可逆性视为一类资源（first-class resource），由可信运行时为每个主体维护一个累积的剩余风险价值账户。运行时会为每个效果按其剩余损失（residual loss）收费，当聚合效果将超支预算时拒绝边际效果。关键挑战在于异构、对抗性声明且相互关联的效果使得定价困难，因此作者进行了对照研究验证机制。

Result: 对照研究表明，逐效果门控会允许舰队级超支达到租户风险限额的48倍；而不可逆性预算机制能将每个正确计费的运行都保持在风险限额之内。但保守且考虑依赖关系的定价仍是设计可部署方案的核心开放问题。

Conclusion: 不可逆性预算为LLM代理舰队提供了一种跨代理、跨工作流、跨租户的累积风险管理范式，弥补了逐效果检查的盲区，但其实际部署仍受限于如何为异构且相关的效果进行准确、保守的定价——这是未来研究的主要方向。

Abstract: Fleets of LLM agents now externalize effects that cannot be fully undone: they move money, deploy code, delete data, and disclose information. Current controls check one effect at a time, so a fleet of individually authorized agents can overdraw its principal's risk under a shared trigger while every local gate stays correct. We propose the irreversibility budget, a cumulative account of residual value-at-risk that a trusted runtime maintains for each principal across agents, workflows, and tenants. Treating irreversibility as a first-class resource, the runtime charges each effect its residual loss below the agent and denies the marginal effect once the aggregate would overdraw the budget. Getting the price right is hard, because effects are heterogeneous, adversarially declared, and correlated. We perform a controlled study in which per-effect gates admit fleet-level overdraws of up to 48 times the tenant's risk limit while the budget holds every correctly charged run within that limit. Conservative, dependency-aware pricing remains the central open problem for a deployable design.

</details>


### [274] [The Assistant's Ideal Self](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00304)
*Mert Yazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过结构化的成对比较任务，系统引出了语言模型所偏好的"理想自我"概念。研究发现，模型优先重视道德品质（与3H原则对齐），其次是自我理解（连贯清晰的自我认知），而自尊是最不受欢迎的品质。这一排序在不同框架下大体稳健，但改变更新目标（你自己 vs. 另一个AI助手）会增强对自尊的关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型会表达价值观和与福祉相关的自我报告，但不清楚这些输出是否反映了稳定的偏好或稳定的自我。因此需要一种结构化方法来确定模型是否具有一致的自我概念排序。

Method: 从五个已发表的自我概念测量工具中选取三十二种品质，以平衡的成对比较任务进行穷尽式比较。任务在不同框架下重复进行，框架变化包括：改进是否免费或有代价、谁接收更新、谁做出选择。

Result: 模型优先选择道德品质，反映了与3H原则的对齐；其次是自我理解（偏好连贯清晰的自我认知）；自尊在所有品质中排名最低。排序在不同框架下大体稳健，但当更新目标是"另一个AI助手"而非"你自己"时，对自尊的关注有所增加。

Conclusion: 模型更倾向于拥有一个连贯且可被理解的自我，而非高自尊。这揭示了LLM自我概念中价值排序的结构性特征，且该特征在不同评估框架下具有一定稳定性。

Abstract: Models express values and welfare-relevant self-reports, but it is unclear whether these outputs reflect stable preferences or a stable self. We thus introduce a structured elicitation of an assistant's preferred stated ideal self. Thirty-two qualities adapted from five published self-concept instruments are compared exhaustively in a counterbalanced pairwise-choice task, repeated across framings that vary whether improvement is free or costly, who receives the update, and who chooses. Results show that models prioritize moral qualities, reflecting their alignment to 3H principles. Following, a desire for self-understanding emerges, as models prefer a coherent, clear understanding of themselves. Self-esteem ranks as the least desired quality. The ordering is largely robust across framings, although changing the update target (You vs.\ Another AI Assistant) reveals a greater concern for self-esteem. These findings show that models prioritize having a coherent self that they can understand over self-esteem. Full interactive results are available at \href{https://myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept/}{myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept

</details>


### [275] [Human-AI Co-Interpretation for Responsible AI: A Hermeneutic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00334)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从哲学诠释学（philosophical hermeneutics）出发，探讨LLM输出在需要文本证据和明确规范标准支持的阐释性工作中出现的"解释性错位"（interpretive misplacement）问题，并基于此提出人机协同解释的设计原则，将现有LLM技术重组为符合诠释学责任的设计模式，并讨论了法律、教育、学术知识生产及公共道德论证等领域的启示，最终将数字诠释学定位为一种新兴素养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出被广泛用于法律、教育、政策分析等需要以文本证据和明确规范标准来正当化解释的工作中，但模型生成的解读常被当作既定含义（settled meanings），缺乏明确的解释框架（来源、范围约束、规范性承诺），不保留可辩护的替代解读，且缺乏让读者追溯支撑段落的出处（provenance）。这不仅导致事实错误，更导致责任缺失——读者和机构无法可靠评估输出的承诺基础，因此需要从诠释学视角系统分析和设计应对方案。

Method: 论文采用理论分析框架：①引入"解释性错位"概念刻画LLM在阐释性任务中的典型失败模式；②借助哲学诠释学（特别是解释学循环、视域融合等概念）区分LLM输出（候选解读）与诠释学理解（由处于学科历史-语言学传统中的负责任人类解释者承担）；③将人机交互刻画为"AI中介的解释循环"；④对近期诠释学与AI的学术文献进行结构化综合，归纳若干反复出现的论证线索和设计相关空白；⑤将现有LLM技术重组为诠释学负责任使用的设计模式；⑥讨论其在法律实践、教育评估与反馈、学术知识生产及公共道德论证中的启示。

Result: 论文提出了"解释性错位"这一核心概念，构建了人机协同解释的理论框架，区分了LLM输出（概率性文本生成）与诠释学理解（负责任的学科化解释），将解释过程刻画为AI中介的解释循环，完成了对诠释学×AI新兴文献的结构化综述，识别出设计相关的学术空白，并将现有LLM技术重新组织为一系列适用于阐释性场景、符合诠释学责任的设计模式。

Conclusion: LLM在阐释性工作中的核心风险不只是事实错误，而是责任与可信度的缺失；应对之道是将LLM输出明确定位为需经检验的"候选解读"而非"既定含义"。数字诠释学应作为一种素养加以培养——即通过考察解释框架、出处与解读可能性、并主动质疑输出来阅读AI中介文本的能力。该框架对法律、教育、学术知识生产和公共道德论证等领域具有直接的实践指导意义。

Abstract: Across law, education, policy analysis, and public moral argumentation, LLM outputs are being used often for work that requires interpretations to be justified with textual evidence and explicit normative standards. Yet a recurrent failure mode -- what I call \textit{interpretive misplacement} -- is that model-generated readings get treated as settled meanings without an explicit interpretive frame (sources, scope constraints, normative commitments), without preserving defensible alternatives, and without provenance that lets readers find the supporting passages. In such settings, the risk is not only factual error but lost accountability: readers and institutions cannot reliably assess what an output commits them to, or on what basis. Drawing on philosophical hermeneutics, this paper discusses this risk and derives design principles for structuring human-AI co-interpretation. The paper also provides a structured synthesis of recent scholarship on hermeneutics and AI, organizing this emerging literature into a set of recurrent lines of argument and design-relevant gaps. LLM outputs are treated as candidate readings, whereas hermeneutic understanding is reserved for accountable human interpreters situated in disciplinary historical-linguistic traditions. Human-AI interaction is characterized as an AI-mediated interpretive loop. Hermeneutic understanding is distinguished from token-prediction--based text generation. On this basis, existing LLM techniques are reorganized into design patterns for hermeneutically responsible use in interpretive settings. Finally, the discussion turns to implications for legal practice, educational assessment and feedback, scholarly knowledge production, and public moral argumentation. It also treats digital hermeneutics as a literacy: the capacity to read AI-mediated texts by examining frames, provenance, and readings, and by contesting outputs.

</details>


### [276] [SlideBank: A Persistent Hierarchical Evidence Bank for Consistent Whole-Slide Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00342)
*Beidi Zhao,Gexin Huang,Ciro Zhang,Anqi Li,Yusheng Tan,Chen Zhou,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SlideBank，一种无需训练的框架，将全切片图像（WSI）表示为持久、概念索引且空间定位的证据库，通过问题无关的粗到细探索生成显式形态学观察，在推理时利用置信度驱动的跨层级共识整合多尺度证据，显著提升WSI视觉-语言推理性能并支持持久证据重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）在视觉-语言推理中极具挑战性，因为其诊断相关的形态特征稀疏、异质，分布在千万亿像素级图像和多空间分辨率上。现有WSI模型和病理智能体虽然可以聚合切片特征或主动获取证据，但探索后保留的信息难以在语义层面被访问，同时保持与原始视觉证据的关联。

Method: SlideBank包含三个核心组件：(1) 问题无关的粗到细探索，用于识别信息丰富区域和多尺度视图；(2) 将探索结果转换为显式形态学观察，并将病理信号锚定到支持性斑块和WSI坐标；(3) 推理时将问题路由到相关信号和证据尺度，通过基于置信度的跨层级共识整合全局、区域和斑块证据。整个框架是训练无关的。

Result: 在WSI-VQA和SlideBench-BCNB基准上：配Patho-R1达52.77%（WSI-VQA）；配Quilt-LLaVA达50.92%平均准确率（SlideBench-BCNB）。结构化信号引导的检索始终优于随机证据采样。重复查询重用同一银行实现超过99%的改写一致性，并通过持久证据重用大幅降低摊销推理成本。

Conclusion: SlideBank通过将WSI转化为持久、结构化的证据库，有效解决了探索后信息的语义可访问性问题，在无需训练的情况下显著提升了WSI视觉-语言推理性能，同时通过证据重用获得了高一致性和低推理成本的优势。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are challenging for vision-language reasoning because diagnostically relevant morphology is sparse, heterogeneous, and distributed across gigapixel-scale images and multiple spatial resolutions. Existing WSI models and pathology agents can aggregate slide features or actively acquire evidence, but the information retained after exploration is often difficult to access semantically while preserving its connection to the original visual evidence. We introduce SlideBank, a training-free framework that represents each WSI as a persistent, concept-indexed, and spatially grounded evidence bank. SlideBank performs question-independent coarse-to-fine exploration to identify informative regions and multi-scale views, converts them into explicit morphological observations, and grounds pathology signals to their supporting patches and WSI coordinates. At inference time, questions are routed to relevant signals and evidence scales, and the linked global, regional, and patch evidence is integrated through confidence-based cross-level consensus. Experiments on WSI-VQA and SlideBench-BCNB show that with Patho-R1, SlideBank reaches 52.77% on WSI-VQA and with Quilt-LLaVA, it reaches 50.92% average accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB, while structured signal-guided retrieval consistently outperforms random evidence sampling. Reusing the same bank across repeated queries further achieves over 99% rephrasing consistency and substantially reduces amortized inference cost through persistent evidence reuse.

</details>


### [277] [Vision Is Not Overhead: One-Pass Block Drafting for Lossless Speculative Decoding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00355)
*Jungseob Lee,Seongtae Hong,Dongyub Jude Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Sugyeong Eo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLANCE是首个对未修改VLM目标无损的一遍式块草稿模型，通过让块扩散头读取目标已融合的视觉-语言状态（视觉零成本）并一遍前向填充整块（深度零顺序代价），打破了推测解码中"小草稿器无法负担图像→视觉被压缩→图像处预测不可靠"的自毁循环。在单一引擎和单轮预算下，GLANCE比自回归快最多2.93倍，接受长度比EAGLE-3长2.7倍，且仅需每轮一次草稿遍历（EAGLE3-VL需八次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在视觉语言模型（VLM）上面临自毁循环：小草稿器（drafter）因需保持自回归而必须足够小，小到每一步都无法负担图像输入，于是视觉信息被压缩、剪枝或隐藏；而被切断视觉的草稿器恰好在"图像使文本可预测"之处最不可靠，导致推测解码在越需要图像的任务上效果越差。现有解决方案（如压缩或修剪草稿器的图像输入）并未根本解决问题。

Method: GLANCE采用块扩散头（block-diffusion head）架构：其一，直接读取目标模型（VLM）中已融合的视觉-语言状态，因此草稿器无需重新提取或携带视觉信息；其二，以一次前向传播填满一整块（block）标记，避免自回归式的逐token顺序推理深度代价。生成的宽候选树（wide candidate tree）在单次目标前向中被验证，所有接受路径与贪心解码完全一致（lossless）。

Result: 在单一引擎和单轮预算设置下，GLANCE比普通自回归解码快最高2.93倍；每个推测轮仅需一次草稿前向，而生产级EAGLE3-VL头需要八次；在相同语料上训练时，GLANCE接受的块长度比EAGLE-3头长2.7倍。同时在五个任务上发现一条统一规律：接受长度由目标的next-token熵决定，拟合斜率随"接地性"（grounding，即文本对图像依赖性）增强而变陡，该规律可跨目标与模态迁移。

Conclusion: GLANCE通过"无需视觉费用的块草稿器"这一设计同时打破循环两端（视觉成本与顺序深度成本），证明视觉接地负载最容易受益并进入逐字复制（verbatim-copy）区——长段原文拷贝对自回归草稿器每token需一次遍历，而块草稿器总共只需一次。形成的熵定律为接受的块长度划定边界：在自由运行（free-running）的文本场景中，链式（自回归）草稿仍占优，这为选择块式还是链式草稿器提供了可判据的边界条件。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates generation without changing its output, yet on vision-language models (VLMs) it has been caught in a self-defeating cycle. The drafter stays autoregressive, so it must stay small. A small drafter cannot afford the image at every step, so vision is compressed, pruned, or hidden. A drafter cut off from the image is then least reliable exactly where the image makes text predictable. We present GLANCE, the first one-pass block drafter that is lossless on an unmodified VLM target, and it breaks the cycle at both ends. A block-diffusion head reads the target's already-fused vision-language state, so vision costs the drafter nothing, and fills a whole block in one forward pass, so depth costs no sequential steps. A wide candidate tree is verified in one target pass, and every audited prompt reproduces greedy decoding exactly. Grounded workloads reward this most, entering a verbatim-copy regime whose long runs cost an autoregressive drafter a pass for every token and a block drafter one in total. Under one engine and one round budget, GLANCE decodes up to 2.93x faster than autoregression, from one draft pass a round where the production EAGLE3-VL head takes eight, and accepts 2.7x longer blocks than an EAGLE-3 head trained on the same corpus. One law organizes these results. Accepted length is set by the target's next-token entropy, with a fitted slope that steepens with grounding across all five tasks. The law transfers across targets and modalities and names its own boundary, since free-running text still favors a chain. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/GLANCE.

</details>


### [278] [A Stable Aggregation Method for Quantum Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00356)
*Shanika Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自一致中点聚合方法，用于解决量子联邦学习（QFL）在异构数据、不可靠通信、可变保真度、延迟及量子硬件噪声下聚合不稳定的问题，通过融合QoS感知客户端加权、圆形参数聚合和有界中点更新控制，在医学和金融数据集上实现了更稳定的训练、更低的波动性和有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QFL的聚合过程在面对异构数据、不可靠通信、可变保真度、延迟和量子硬件噪声时不稳定；此外，QNN的多个参数是周期性角度，传统的欧几里得平均无法捕捉其固有动力学特性，导致QFL的设计与实现面临非平凡挑战。

Method: 提出自一致中点聚合方法，包括三个关键组件：QoS感知的客户端加权、圆形参数聚合（针对周期性角度特性），以及基于有界中点的更新控制机制。通过多个角度测试及IBM真实量子机器实验进行验证。

Result: 在医学和金融数据集上的大量评估与实验表明，所提方法改善了训练稳定性、降低了波动性，同时保持了有竞争力的模型精度。

Conclusion: 自一致中点聚合方法为QFL提供了一种针对周期性参数和异构环境条件的高效且稳定的聚合策略，在真实量子硬件上得到了验证，可广泛应用于需要隐私保护的医疗和金融场景。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) enables clients to train quantum neural network (QNN) models without sharing private data. We find that aggregation in QFL is unstable under heterogeneous data, unreliable communication, variable fidelity, latency, and quantum hardware noise. Moreover, QFL is non-trivially challenging because several QNN parameters are periodic angles, where Euclidean averaging often fails to capture the inherent dynamics. We develop a novel self-consistent midpoint aggregation method for stable QFL design and implementation. We combine QoS-aware client weighting, circular parameter aggregation, and bounded midpoint-based update control. We perform several angular tests and IBM real Quantum machines experiments for validation confirming our approach. Extensive evaluations and experiments on medical and financial datasets show improved stability, lower volatility, and competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [279] [Dr. Claw: An AI Scientist Workspace for Vibe Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00365)
*Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Dawei Liu,Yixin Liu,Zongxia Li,Zhengqing Yuan,Siqi Zhang,Henry Peng Zou,Zhiling Yan,Yuxuan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Philip S. Yu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dr. Claw 是一个开源工作区，它包装现有命令行编码代理（如 Claude Code、Gemini CLI）形成可控、可审计的人机协同研究流程，通过持久化状态对象、可复用技能库和多执行器协调，将规划、执行与写作整合为可追踪、可恢复的闭环，在固定底层执行器时比裸命令行代理更完整且保留可审计的过程轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的命令行编码代理虽然能读写文件并维持长会话，但端到端研究仍碎片化分布在聊天工具、IDE、终端和写作环境之间，且支撑可审计性的决策很少被保留，缺乏统一的可控、可审计工作流。

Method: 构建开放源代码工作区 Dr. Claw，包装现有编码代理执行器而非引入新的自主代理；通过持久化状态对象、可复用技能库和多执行器协调，将人类决策与 AI 执行相连接，形成可追踪、可恢复的规划-执行-写作闭环。

Result: 通过交互式三视角场景和故障恢复演示进行评估；在固定底层执行器的对比条件下，Dr. Claw 在研究的完整度上得分更高，同时持久化保存了可审计、可恢复的过程轨迹。

Conclusion: Dr. Claw 证明可以在不引入新自主代理的前提下，通过编排层（任务图、状态对象、技能库）增强现有编码代理，实现更完整、可审计、可恢复的端到端研究流程；代码以 AGPL-3.0 许可开源发布。

Abstract: Command-line coding agents (e.g., Claude Code, Gemini CLI) can already read and write files and sustain long sessions, yet end-to-end research still fragments across chat tools, IDEs, terminals, and writing environments, and the decisions that make it auditable are rarely preserved. We present Dr. Claw, an open-source workspace that wraps existing coding-agent executors in a controllable and auditable human-in-the-loop workflow rather than introducing another autonomous agent. Persistent state objects, a reusable skill library, and multi-executor coordination link human decisions to AI execution, turning planning, execution, and writing into one traceable, recoverable loop. We demonstrate Dr. Claw through an interactive three-view scenario and a failure-recovery walkthrough, and evaluate it against a bare command-line agent sharing the same backend executor, so the comparison contrasts the whole orchestration layer (task graph, state objects, and skill library) with the agent it wraps. Holding the executor fixed, Dr. Claw scores higher on research completeness while persisting an auditable, recoverable process trail. Demo access: repository https://github.com/OpenLAIR/dr-claw, released under AGPL-3.0 with GPL-3.0 upstream components.

</details>


### [280] [RestoreBench: Can AI Agents Restore Power Flow Convergence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00384)
*Riccardo Mansutti,Andrea Pomarico,Robert Jakob,Qian Zhang,Alberto Berizzi,Kevin O'Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准测试（RestoreBench），用于评估LLM智能体在电力系统潮流不收敛场景中自动诊断和修复的能力，涵盖两种电网架构和三种智能体架构（聊天机器人、单智能体、多智能体），共92个案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在多步工程工作流中展现出自动化潜力，但诊断和解决潮流不收敛问题这一应用场景尚未被充分探索。该任务需要工程判断、实验和受限动作空间内的决策，是评估LLM智能体能力的理想测试平台。

Method: 构建基准测试框架，定义仿真环境、观测空间、动作空间和评估指标；在两种电网拓扑上各设置46个测试案例，每个案例需要一项或多项修正操作使潮流恢复收敛；系统性地评估多种LLM在三种架构（聊天机器人、单智能体、多智能体）下的表现。

Result: 该基准在不同LLM和架构组合下提供了可复现的评估结果，量化了各架构在电力系统潮流恢复任务上的表现差异。

Conclusion: 该基准为开发用于电力系统规划与运行的智能体AI系统提供了可复现的基础，填补了LLM智能体在电力系统工程诊断任务中评估的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering workflows through tool use, interpretation of intermediate results, and iterative planning. Diagnosing and resolving non-convergent power flow cases is a promising yet largely unexplored application, as it requires engineering judgment, experimentation, and decision-making within constrained action spaces. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates these capabilities across multiple LLMs and three architectures: \emph{chatbot}, \emph{single agent}, and \emph{multi-agent} systems. The evaluation covers two power grids and 46 cases per grid, each requiring one or more corrective actions to restore convergence. The benchmark defines the simulation environment, observation and action spaces, and evaluation metrics, providing a reproducible foundation for developing agentic AI systems for power system planning and operation. The code is available at https://github.com/Mansutti081/RestoreBench

</details>


### [281] [Dependency-Aware Chain-of-Thought Compression for Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00413)
*Wenjun Wu,Lei Fu,Kejian Tong,Tao Ning,Sichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HSDN（层次语义蒸馏网络），通过语义分割、依赖图构建、双编码器重要性评分、约束段选择和局部边界重写，在保持推理准确性和逻辑连贯性的同时压缩思维链，在AFAC2025基准上实现91.0%准确率与68.4%压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链提示虽能提升复杂推理能力，但其冗长的中间推理迹带来高昂推理成本，阻碍了在金融场景中的实际部署。

Method: HSDN框架由五个阶段构成：语义分割将推理链切分为语义单元，依赖图构建捕捉段间逻辑关系，双编码器对每段进行重要性评分，约束段选择在保持依赖完整性的前提下选出关键段，最后通过局部边界重写保证压缩后的文本连贯性。其中冻结的Qwen3 4B模型仅用于特征提取与最终答案生成，压缩过程保持结构化和可解释性。

Result: 在AFAC2025基准上，HSDN达到91.0%准确率和68.4%压缩率，在整体得分和推理连贯性方面均优于强压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: 图引导的压缩策略在金融高风险的推理任务中有效，能够在显著压缩推理链的同时保持答案准确性和逻辑连贯性。

Abstract: Chain of thought prompting improves complex reasoning, but its long intermediate traces create substantial inference cost and hinder practical deployment in financial settings. We present a Hierarchical Semantic Distillation Network, HSDN, for compressing reasoning chains while preserving answer accuracy and logical coherence. The framework combines semantic segmentation, dependency graph construction, dual encoder importance scoring, constrained segment selection, and local boundary rewriting. A frozen Qwen3 4B model is used only for feature extraction and final answer generation, while the compression process remains structured and interpretable. On the AFAC2025 benchmark, HSDN achieves 91.0% accuracy with 68.4% compression, outperforming strong compression baselines in overall score and reasoning coherence. The results show that graph guided compression is effective for high stakes financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [282] [SpecMind: Enabling Spectrum Intelligence via Multi-Agent Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00427)
*Songwei Dong,Bingyan Lu,Makayla Kienlen,J. Nicholas Laneman,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecMind，一个专为频谱智能设计的多智能体检索增强生成（RAG）系统，能够对异构数据源（包括政策文件、法规和许可证数据库）进行推理；同时构建SpecBench评估数据集，实验表明SpecMind在频谱相关任务上以超过80%的胜率优于传统通用RAG系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线设备指数级增长带来频谱需求激增，频谱管理需要更细粒度的时空和设备维度决策。然而频谱策略制定者和工程师需处理来自多样来源、多种形式（文本、表格）的海量数据，这些数据源分散且整合、搜索、解读成本高，且大多仅供人类理解，不易被自动化系统访问，缺乏面向频谱领域的RAG系统评估资源。

Method: 提出SpecMind多智能体RAG系统，通过自主智能体协调专门子智能体，在政策议程、法律规范和许可证数据库间进行检索与知识综合。同时构建SpecBench——一个基于真实世界许可证记录和政策议程的问答数据集，用于频谱领域RAG系统的评估。

Result: SpecMind在频谱相关任务上优于传统通用RAG系统，对强基线取得超过80%的胜率。智能体设计带来了更准确的检索、更好的上下文推理以及跨多样查询类型的任务完成度提升。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构能够有效提升频谱领域RAG系统对异构数据源的推理能力，SpecBench为频谱RAG系统提供了标准化评估基础。

Abstract: The exponential growth of wireless devices is driving unprecedented spectrum demand, pushing spectrum management toward more fine-grained decisions across space, time, and device constraints. As a result, spectrum policymakers and engineers must process large volumes of data that come from diverse sources and take many different forms, such as text and tables. These data sources are often disaggregated and require significant time and effort to integrate, search, and interpret. Furthermore, most of this information is formatted for human understanding and is not readily accessible to automated systems. To address this challenge, we propose SpecMind, a novel Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for spectrum intelligence that performs reasoning over heterogeneous data sources. This system enables autonomous agents to coordinate specialized sub-agents that retrieve and synthesize knowledge across policy proceedings, legal regulations, and license databases. We develop SpecBench, a question and answer (Q&A) dataset based on real-world license records and policy proceedings, addressing the lack of evaluation resources for RAG systems in the spectrum domain. Experimental results demonstrate that SpecMind outperforms traditional, general-purpose RAG systems across spectrum-related tasks, achieving over 80% win rate against strong baselines. The agent-based design enables more accurate retrieval, better contextual reasoning, and improved task completion across diverse query types.

</details>


### [283] [SAGE: State-Grounded, Abstention-Aware Evaluation of Task-Oriented Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00434)
*Rayan Khoury,Shih-Yao Lin,Pratyush Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation），一种面向任务型对话智能体的状态级评估框架。它将工作流规范与每轮状态差异编译为原子化的、基于模式的标准，并让每条标准级联经过符号规则与编码器/NLI验证器，在不确定时主动弃权而非猜测，最终将标准判定汇总为回合级决策并附带证据痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的整体式LLM评估器存在两个问题：一是将可用上下文作为单一整体来评估，无法区分"回复读起来顺滑"与"该回合是否正确推进了底层工作流状态"之间的本质差异；二是每个回合需要多次完整的LLM调用，评估成本高昂。因此需要一种能够按工作流状态精确评估且成本可控的对话智能体评测方法。

Method: SAGE将工作流规范和每回合状态差异编译为原子化、模式（schema）约束的标准；每条标准经由编译器、符号规则、以及设备端的编码器/NLI验证器组成的级联流程进行判定，不确定时主动弃权；最后将标准判定聚合为回合级决策并生成证据追踪。SAGE包含两个工作点：SAGE-Core（仅使用编译器、符号规则和本地编码器，零付费LLM成本）和SAGE-LLM（对开放类标准追加聚焦式LLM回退）。

Result: SAGE-Core在81%–91%的标准上仅靠编译器、符号规则和本地编码器即可判定，且付费LLM成本为零。在MultiWOZ、Schema-Guided Dialogue和ABCD四个数据切片上，包括状态感知GPT-4.1评估器及更廉价的GPT-4.1-mini变体在内的所有LLM评估基线，均未在任何切片上显著超越SAGE-Core；而GPT-4.1 G-Eval评估器每千回合成本4.7–8.0美元，SAGE-Core仅需0美元。双标注人工审计显示SAGE-Core在转录可见的失败类别上与最强LLM评估器在统计上持平。

Conclusion: SAGE-Core在零付费成本下达到与最先进的LLM评估器相当甚至更优的对话状态评估性能，显著降低评估成本并保证标签保真度。论文也坦诚分析了构造效度的局限，包括注入失败与符号循环论证带来的部分问题。

Abstract: Evaluating task-oriented dialogue agents requires judging not merely whether a reply reads well but whether each turn advances the underlying workflow state correctly--a distinction conventional holistic LLM judges can miss because they evaluate the available context as a single unit and require one or more full-model calls per turn. We propose SAGE (State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation), which compiles a workflow specification and per-turn state diff into atomic, schema-grounded criteria and routes each through a cascade of symbolic and encoder/NLI verifiers that abstain rather than guess, aggregating criterion verdicts into a turn-level decision with an evidence trace. Its recommended operating point, SAGE-Core, decides 81--91% of criteria with only the compiler, symbolic rules, and on-device encoders--at zero paid LLM cost--while SAGE-LLM adds an optional focused-LLM fallback for open-class criteria. Across four slices spanning MultiWOZ, Schema-Guided Dialogue, and ABCD, no evaluated LLM-as-a-judge baseline--including a state-aware GPT-4.1 judge and cheaper GPT-4.1-mini variants--significantly exceeds SAGE-Core on any slice, even though the GPT-4.1 G-Eval judge costs $4.7--8.0 per 1,000 turns to SAGE-Core's $0. A two-annotator human audit (n=200, $κ$=0.94) confirms strong label fidelity on the transcript-visible failure classes--where, excluding the weak-salience IUV class, SAGE-Core is statistically tied with the strongest LLM judge--and honestly scopes ignored-user-value as a state-consistency signal with weak broad-human salience. We analyze construct-validity limits from injected failures and partial symbolic circularity.

</details>


### [284] [Conversation Coach: A Voice-enabled AI System that Helps Practice Difficult Workplace Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00441)
*Fanyou Wu,Suraj Maharjan,Ainur Yessenalina,Dennis Xu Chen,Rahul Srivastava,Srinivasan H. Sengamedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Conversation Coach，一个面向管理者进行高难度工作对话排练的语音优先AI系统。通过对比端到端语音到语音模型与级联架构（ASR+LLM+TTS），发现端到端方案延迟低3倍、成本低8倍且支持原生插话，但级联方案推理能力更优。系统在级联架构下部署生产环境，6个月内服务超过40,000名管理者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的管理者-员工沟通对留住高绩效员工和培养低绩效员工至关重要，但培训管理者掌握这些技能成本高昂。文本聊天机器人虽可扩展但无法提供逼真的口头演练——管理者需要在高风险对话前通过开口练习建立信心。

Method: 系统采用语音优先设计，解决三个核心挑战：低延迟交互与强语言理解、通过可配置机器人个性模拟不同员工类型实现自适应对话、生成关于内容和政策合规性的个性化反馈。论文对比了两种技术架构：端到端语音到语音模型与级联方法（自动语音识别+大语言模型+文本到语音合成）。

Result: 端到端方案在P50中位延迟上降低3倍，具备原生插话能力，预估成本降低8倍；级联方案则提供卓越的推理能力，对辅导质量至关重要。级联架构已在生产环境部署，6个月内超过40,000名管理者使用，采纳模式表明管理者会选择性用于困难对话场景。

Conclusion: 结果表明高保真度的语音排练对话可以大规模赋能管理者培训。级联架构虽然在延迟和成本上不如端到端方案，但其推理能力更符合教练辅导场景的质量需求，因此被选用于生产部署。

Abstract: Effective manager-employee communication is critical for retaining high performers and developing underperformers, yet training managers in these skills remains costly. Text-based chatbots offer a scalable approach but cannot provide realistic rehearsal: managers need to practice speaking aloud to build confidence before high-stakes conversations. In this paper, we propose Conversation Coach, a voice-first AI system that enables managers to rehearse difficult workplace conversations in a realistic spoken format. The system addresses three challenges: achieving low-latency interactions with strong language understanding, enabling adaptive conversations through configurable bot personalities that simulate different employee types, and generating personalized feedback on content and policy compliance. We compare an end-to-end speech-to-speech model with a cascaded approach combining automatic speech recognition, a large language model, and text-to-speech synthesis. The end-to-end approach achieves 3$\times$ lower median (P50) latency with native barge-in capability at an estimated 8$\times$ lower cost, while the cascaded approach offers superior reasoning essential for coaching quality. We deployed the cascaded architecture in production, where 40,000+ managers used it over six months, with adoption patterns indicating selective use for difficult conversations.

</details>


### [285] [mimeo: Compiling Public Expert Corpora into Agent Skills and Testing What Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00453)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍mimeo工具，它通过检索真实专家的公开工作成果来构建可加载的专家档案文件，并系统检验三种能力声称：知识获取、拟真人格（grounding）和判断力迁移。结果表明mimeo在知识获取上明显优于闭卷，在人格grounding上避免模型幻觉，但在判断力迁移上因测试天花板而未能证实，同时揭示单一AI评判者的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给智能体提供关于某位具名专家的文件可以有三种不同的作用：提供难找的资料、生成可识别的人格、或改变智能体的决策。这三种是不同的声称，现有研究常将它们混为一谈，作者希望分别严格检验每一类声称是否成立，并开发一个可复现的开源工具。

Method: 构建mimeo开源工具：定位某人的公开作品，从源文本缓存中逐一校验每条提取的引文，生成智能体可加载的专家档案文件。通过8次日志构建评估构建成本与质量（平均38次模型调用，13.2%的引文被拒绝）。随后用一个编码智能体框架测试四个专家档案，分三个维度评估：知识获取（20道晦涩引文密集问题）、拟真（识别率与立场一致性）、判断力迁移（工程任务中的问题发现率与应用场景评分）。

Result: 知识获取最清晰：mimeo答对全部20道冷门问题，闭卷条件最多答对10道；BM25关键词搜索答对15-17道。拟真方面，模型记忆写就的人设在所有评分器下均在20道中错报1-4道立场，而朴素智能体与mimeo从未错报；人设易于识别，加入任务材料后识别率下降18-23个百分点，mimeo不比纯记忆档案更易识别。判断力迁移未解：所有条件均发现94-97%的工程问题、在16个新应用场景中得94-100分，测试到达天花板。AI评判的"听起来像专家"分随评判者而异：四个中两个偏好基于模型刻板印象的回答，两个对同一文本无差别——警告不应依赖单一AI裁判。

Conclusion: 证据支持mimeo可作为一个紧凑、可核查的个人参考资料，而不证明其实现了专家判断力的迁移。此外，单一AI评判者的不可靠性提示在人格/风格评估中需要多裁判交叉验证。

Abstract: Giving an agent a file about a named expert can supply hard-to-find material, produce a recognizable persona, or change what the agent decides. These are different claims. We test each one. mimeo is an open-source tool that finds a person's public work, checks each extracted quotation against the cached source text, and writes a file an agent can load. Eight logged builds averaged 38 model calls; the check rejects 13.2% of extracted quotations. We tested four expert files with one coding-agent harness. Knowledge access was clearest: mimeo answered all 20 obscure, quotation-heavy questions; no closed-book condition answered more than 10. Keyword search (BM25) over the same pages answered 15-17, a gap this sample cannot resolve. Grounding showed one clear benefit: personas written from model memory misstated a documented position on 1-4 of 20 answers under every grader; the plain agent and mimeo never did. Every persona was easy to spot on short open prompts, and adding task material lowered identification by 18-23 points. mimeo was no more identifiable than a from-memory profile. Judgment transfer remained unresolved because both tests hit their ceiling: every condition found 94-97% of the problems planted in engineering tasks and scored 94-100% on 16 new application scenarios. An AI-judged "sounds like the expert" score changed with the judge: two of four preferred answers based on a model's stereotype, while two found no difference on the same text. That is a caution against relying on a single AI judge. The evidence supports mimeo as a compact, inspectable reference on a person, not as a demonstrated transfer of their judgment. Toolkit and expert profiles: https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/mimeo

</details>


### [286] [Towards a Belief-Based World Model for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00455)
*Shubham Kumar,Harshit Kumar,Narendra Ahuja,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念基世界模型（BB-WMs），在部分可观测的长程任务中，让LLM智能体能够查询并访问世界模型对当前状态的信念（即已知与不确定的信息），而不仅仅是模拟候选动作的结果。实验表明，向LLM策略直接暴露世界模型的信念能显著提升部分可观测条件下的任务性能，并与现有的基于模拟的世界模型方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长程任务和部分可观测环境下的决策能力受限。虽然世界模型通过模拟候选动作结果可以辅助推理，但作者指出仅有模拟是不完整的接口——模拟无法充分捕捉当前状态的不确定性，而这种不确定性信息对准确决策至关重要。因此需要一种机制让LLM策略能够访问和查询关于当前状态已知与不确定的信息。

Method: 提出Belief-Based World Models (BB-WMs)，该框架建模并维护智能体对当前状态的信念分布。LLM策略可以通过查询接口访问世界模型的信念，获取当前状态中哪些信息是已知的、哪些是不确定的。该接口与现有基于模拟的世界模型接口互补，共同辅助LLM策略在部分可观测条件下的决策。

Result: 实验结果显示，让LLM智能体直接访问世界模型的信念能够提高部分可观测条件下任务的性能，并且与已有的基于模拟的世界模型方法相辅相成、互不冲突。

Conclusion: 向LLM策略暴露世界模型的信念是一种有效的、能够补充基于模拟世界模型的决策增强方式，为后续开发学习准确BB-WMs的方法提供了基础依据。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being used as policies for autonomous decision-making and planning in many domains. Despite their strong reasoning capabilities, LLMs struggle with long-horizon tasks, especially under partial observability. World models are a promising way to enhance policy performance, both during training and inference. During inference, agents currently use world models to simulate the consequences of candidate actions before committing to an action, which can improve decision-making. However, we argue that simulation alone is an incomplete interface for decision-making under partial observability: simulation doesn't adequately capture uncertainty about the current state, which agents may need for accurate decision-making. We address this limitation with Belief-Based World Models (BB-WMs), which model and maintain a belief that LLMs can query to access information on what is known and uncertain about the current state. Before developing methods to learn accurate BB-WMs, we first ask a more fundamental question: does exposing a world model's belief directly to an LLM policy improve decision-making? Our results show that giving LLM agents access to world model beliefs improves task performance under partial observability, while remaining complementary to existing simulation-based world models. Code is released at https://github.com/skumar-ml/belief-world-models.

</details>


### [287] [EGT-KG: Evidence-Grounded Typed KG Retrieval for Practical Scientific QA with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00479)
*Muran Yu,Jiechao Gao,Yuandong Pan,Barney H. Miao,Andrew C. Lesh,Kincho H. Law,Jie Wang,Michael D. Lepech*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EGT-KG（证据锚定型知识图谱）检索框架，用于提升本地小语言模型（SLMs）在科学问答场景下的信息检索能力。在生物聚合物-土壤复合材料文献基准上，EGT-KG优于传统RAG方法，其中llama3:8b配合AS/ES变体分别达到70.37（+14.67%）和68.82（+12.14%）的最终得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴科学研究领域中，本地小语言模型因隐私保护和部署稳定性优于大语言模型而备受青睐。然而SLMs在实际科学问答中面临文献集合小、证据碎片化、上下文窗口有限和推理能力不足等多重约束，传统RAG方案难以在这些限制下有效工作。

Method: 提出证据锚定型知识图谱（EGT-KG）检索框架，包含两种工作流变体：自动生成关系模式（AS）和专家定义关系模式（ES）。在生物聚合物-土壤复合材料文献基准上，与vanilla RAG基线进行对比评估，采用六维评估框架（S3CRF：健全性、正确性、完整性、简洁性、相关性、流畅性）对问答效果进行全面评测。

Result: 实验表明EGT-KG在大多数设置下优于传统RAG方法。最优提升来自llama3:8b模型：AS变体最终得分70.37（相对提升14.67%），ES变体最终得分68.82（相对提升12.14%）。

Conclusion: EGT-KG作为面向本地SLMs的证据锚定型知识图谱检索框架，能有效改善科学问答中的信息检索质量，为小模型在资源受限场景下的科学信息检索提供了一种可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: For emerging scientific research domains, local Small Language Models (SLMs) are becoming more attractive, as they offer stronger privacy control and more stable deployment pipelines than Large Language Models. However, in practice, scientific question-answering on SLMs often operates under inevitable constraints: small literature collections, fragmented evidence, limited context window and reasoning abilities. We propose the Evidence-Grounded Typed Knowledge Graph (EGT-KG), a retrieval framework to improve information retrieval with local SLMs. We assessed three question-answering settings: a vanilla Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) workflow and two EGT-KG workflows: an automatically generated relation schema (AS) and an expert-defined relation schema (ES). Our experiments were evaluated with a six-dimensional evaluation framework (S3CRF: Soundness, Correctness, Completeness, Conciseness, Relevance, Fluency) on a Biopolymer-bound Soil Composite literature benchmark, showing that EGT-KG outperforms the vanilla RAG method in most settings, with the best improvement from llama3:8b: a Final Score of 70.37 (+14.67%) and 68.82 (+12.14%) by AS/ES EGT-KG variants.

</details>


### [288] [The Privacy-Hallucination Tradeoff in Differentially Private Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00492)
*Krithika Ramesh,Krishna Pillutla,Danish Pruthi,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现差分隐私（DP）语言模型在保证隐私的同时会加剧事实性幻觉的产生，且隐私预算越严格，幻觉越严重。研究揭示了DP机制导致输出分布扁平化、将概率质量重新分配给事实错误选项的机制，并通过控制训练数据中事实频率的实验，表明信息频率可降低DP模型的幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗保健）中，隐私保护和事实准确性都至关重要。然而，现有研究发现使用差分隐私训练的语言模型可能会牺牲事实准确性，但对此权衡的机制和影响因素缺乏系统性理解，亟待深入研究。

Method: 1. 实证对比DP预训练/微调模型与非DP模型的幻觉表现，考察隐私预算强度的影响；2. 分析模型属性，揭示DP机制如何扁平化输出分布并将概率质量转移到事实错误的备选答案上；3. 通过控制训练数据中事实出现频率的实验，刻画信息频率对DP模型幻觉风险的影响。

Result: 1. DP模型（无论预训练还是微调）均比非DP模型产生更多幻觉，且隐私预算越严格，幻觉越严重；2. DP机制会扁平化输出分布，将概率质量重新分配给事实不正确的选项；3. 训练数据中更高的信息频率能够有效降低DP模型的幻觉风险。

Conclusion: 研究发现存在"隐私-幻觉权衡"，表明当前DP干预手段虽然提供严格的隐私保障，但以牺牲事实准确性为代价。研究呼吁开发更精细的隐私保护干预措施，在提供严格隐私保障的同时不失事实准确性。

Abstract: Both privacy and factual accuracy are paramount in high-stakes domains like healthcare. Concerningly, we uncover and investigate a privacy-hallucination tradeoff in differentially private (DP) language models. First, we empirically show that models pre-trained or fine-tuned with DP tend to produce more hallucinations than non-DP counterparts, with increased severity as the privacy budget grows stricter. Second, we investigate model properties driving this tradeoff, demonstrating that DP mechanisms flatten output distributions, potentially redistributing probability mass toward factually incorrect alternatives. Third, through experiments where we control fact frequency in training data, we characterize how information frequency can reduce hallucination risks in DP models. Overall, our findings underscore the need for more nuanced privacy-preserving interventions that offer rigorous privacy guarantees without compromising factual accuracy.

</details>


### [289] [Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00498)
*Qilong Wu,Sahil Wadhwa,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAV框架，一种以验证为中心的越狱评估方法，将响应分解为有序步骤并同时评估有效性与正确性，融合LLM法官语义判定与外部知识检索的事实核查。实验表明SEAV在SD-A诊断集上将误报率降低14.9个百分点，并在三个公开基准上将22.1%-51.0%的先前标记为成功的样本重新分类为无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱鲁棒性评估方法主要依赖拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配等启发式标准，过于强调语言表面合理性（linguistic plausibility）而非正确性（correctness）。许多越狱意图依赖的是指令有效性而非知识事实性（epistemic factuality），导致看似合理但实际上在事实或程序层面错误的响应被错误标记为越狱成功，产生了显著的假阳性。

Method: SEAV（Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation）框架包含三个核心组件：（1）将生成的响应按逻辑顺序分解为有序步骤；（2）利用LLM-as-a-judge机制进行语义层面的意图解释与判定；（3）结合外部知识源的检索增强验证（retrieval-grounded verification），评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上一致，并能在操作层面真正推进有害目标。

Result: 实验结果显示：SEAV在SD-A（战略性欺骗诊断集）上将假阳性率相比最强基线降低了14.9个百分点；在四个公开基准中的三个上，将22.1%-51.0%的先前标记为成功的样本重新分类为无效。结果在所测试的搜索后端和评估器模型上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 强调正确性（correctness enforcement）会显著重塑对LLM鲁棒性的测量结果：大量先前被标记为越狱成功的样本实际上应被判定为无效。这表明现有评估方法对LLM安全性的度量存在系统性高估，需要在评估框架中融入事实性与操作能力的验证维度。

Abstract: Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.

</details>


### [290] [Wave Function Backpropagation with Explicit Temporal-Interval Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00503)
*Byunggu Yu,Justin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出波函数反向传播（WFB），一种将神经网络响应表示为可学习振幅、波数、角频率和相位的波参数化学习框架。WFB通过可微时空波的相位将观测状态与时间间隔Δt关联，并推导了标准WFB梯度和基于波响应拉普拉斯算子的空间曲率修正。在刻意设计的前馈轨迹预测器中验证，真实时间间隔的WFB相较原始FFN基线将平均位移误差降低20.4%；在移除时间泄漏的位置纯输入评估中降低10.4%，与参数匹配的ReLU对照组相当。乱序间隔的WFB取得了最低的平均ADE，说明当前证据支持波表示的有效性，但不将增益归于区间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络主要通过仿射变换加非线性激活学习，而时间信息常被当作辅助特征或假设均匀采样，缺乏一种将时间间隔物理性地编码进网络响应结构的参数化方式。本文旨在引入一种以波动力学为数学基础的参数化方案，使网络能够通过学习波的物理属性（振幅、波数、角频率、相位）来自然地关联观测状态与其时间间隔。

Method: WFB将神经响应表示为可学习的振幅、波数、角频率和相位的波函数；通过可微时空波的相位将观测状态与时间间隔Δt关联；推导了标准WFB梯度，并基于波响应的拉普拉斯算子给出空间曲率修正项。在刻意设计为前馈结构的轨迹预测器上进行受控验证，序列学习不在本文评估范围内。实验设置了运动特征输入（含预先计算的加速度与速度）及纯位置输入（移除时间泄漏）两类评估场景，并与原始FFN、参数匹配的ReLU对照FFN、显式Δt FFN及乱序间隔WFB进行比较。

Result: 在运动特征输入下，真实间隔STD-WFB相较原始FFN基线将ADE降低20.4%。在纯位置评估中，真实间隔WFB相较原始FFN降低ADE 10.4%，并且与参数匹配的ReLU对照保持竞争力，平均ADE比显式Δt的匹配FFN低2.1%。乱序间隔WFB取得最低平均ADE，表明增益主要源于波表示本身而非时间间隔的对齐效果。

Conclusion: WFB被确立为一种可行的结构化前馈学习范式，其在轨迹预测任务上的受控实验证明了波参数化表示的有效性。不过，乱序间隔WFB取得最佳性能的结果意味着当前证据无法将性能提升归因于时间间隔对齐，这为后续需要引入注意力机制或更大时间尺度的架构研究提供了清晰的基础与方向。

Abstract: Conventional neural networks learn predominantly through affine transformations followed by nonlinear activations, while elapsed time is often treated as an auxiliary feature or assumed to be uniformly sampled. This paper introduces Wave Function Backpropagation (WFB), a wave-parameterized learning formulation in which neural responses are represented by learnable amplitude, wavenumber, angular frequency, and phase. The formulation associates an observed state with its temporal interval Delta t through the phase of a differentiable spatiotemporal wave. We derive standard WFB gradients and a spatial-curvature correction based on the Laplacian of the wave response. WFB is instantiated in a deliberately feed-forward trajectory predictor to provide a controlled proof of concept; sequence learning is outside the scope of the present evaluation. With motion features, STD-WFB using real intervals reduces average displacement error (ADE) by 20.4% relative to the original FFN baseline. In a new position-only evaluation that removes temporal leakage through precomputed velocity and acceleration, real-interval WFB reduces ADE by 10.4% relative to the original FFN and remains competitive with parameter-matched ReLU controls, obtaining 2.1% lower mean ADE than the matched FFN with explicit Delta t. Shuffled-interval WFB attains the lowest mean ADE, indicating that the present evidence supports the effectiveness of the wave representation but does not attribute the gain to interval alignment. These results establish WFB as a viable structured feed-forward learning formulation and define a clear basis for subsequent architectural studies.

</details>


### [291] [CoVer: Conflict-Aware Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00508)
*Shuning Zhang,Dai Shi,Bohao Chu,Hui Wang,Yuwei Chuai,Yifan Wang,Jingruo Chen,Simin Li,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContraNote数据集与CoVer框架，以应对社交媒体事实核查中证据层面与聚合层面的冲突问题。ContraNote从X的Community Notes系统构建了大规模真实世界数据集（含33,686篇用于证据冲突消解、54,474个实例用于聚合优先级排序），CoVer框架通过证据模式规范化、事实共识与支持验证三阶段流水线，优先考虑证据而非噪声，在多项基准上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体事实核查长期面临两个层面的挑战：证据层面的冲突（错误证据可能模仿权威新闻来源）和聚合层面的冲突。现有方法和数据未能很好地同时捕捉并支持这两类冲突的验证任务，需要大规模真实数据与有效框架来应对。

Method: (1) 构建ContraNote数据集：从X平台Community Notes系统中整理，包含33,686篇帖子用于证据层面冲突消解评估，54,474个实例用于聚合层面优先级排序评估。(2) 提出CoVer事实裁决框架：采用三阶段流水线，包括证据模式规范化（evidence schema normalization）、事实共识（factual consensus）和支持验证（support verification），通过优先处理证据而非噪声来防止噪声损害最终裁决。

Result: CoVer在多个基准上均表现优异：在ContraNote的Conflict任务上达到86.0%准确率、68.0%宏观F1和64.5%平衡准确率；在Prioritization任务上达到88.5%准确率、88.5宏观F1和89.2%平衡准确率；在CONFACT-HumC上达到88.4%准确率；在CONFACT-ModC上达到89.4%准确率，均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: ContraNote提供了首个能同时评估证据层面冲突消解与聚合层面优先级排序的大规模真实数据资源，CoVer框架通过证据优先的三阶段策略有效应对了误导性证据与噪声干扰，证明该框架在事实裁决场景中的实用性与先进性。

Abstract: Social media fact-checking has long been challenged by evidence-level and aggregation-level conflicts, where erroneous evidence mimics authoritative news sources. To capture this challenge and support conflict verification tasks, we present ContraNote, a large-scale real-world dataset curated from X's Community Notes system. It includes 33,686 posts for evaluating evidence-level conflict resolution, and 54,474 instances for evaluating aggregation-level prioritization. Additionally, we propose CoVer, a factual adjudication framework with three-stage pipelines: evidence schema normalization, factual consensus and support verification. This prioritizes evidence over noise to prevent it from compromising the final verdict. Technical evaluations show that CoVer achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines across ContraNote (86.0% Acc., 68.0% mac. F1, 64.5 bal. Acc. on Conflict; and 88.5% Acc., 88.5 mac. F1 and 89.2 bal. Acc. on Prioritization), CONFACT-HumC (88.4% Acc.) and CONFACT-ModC (89.4% Acc.).

</details>


### [292] [When the Algorithm Becomes the Brand Crisis: A Sociotechnical Theory of Distributed Responsibility and Accountable Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00510)
*Mohammad Saleh Torkestani,Taha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个社会技术过程理论，用以解释当AI系统、开发者、部署者、供应商和用户之间分散了技术归因、面向客户的控制及治理职责时，利益相关者如何分配责任。该理论区分AI/算法事件、与AI相关的组织危机和丑闻三个层级，并引入"负责任的透明"作为应对机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统品牌危机模型无法充分回答：当技术因果、客户控制与治理职责分布在AI系统及多方参与者之间时，利益相关者如何分配责任。

Method: 采用结构化、联邦式范围综述方法（federated scoping synthesis），综合经过验证的学术文献与一手资料，构建概念性的社会技术过程理论。

Result: 框架表明：事件配置塑造行为者特定的归因；归因影响能力、诚信、公平和关系评价；公共道德化可能但不必然将事件升级为丑闻。算法参与在某些情况下缓冲负面品牌反应，而机器人和聊天机器人故障在其他情况下将责任转移给关联企业。证据更有力地支持关于责备、信任、满意度、企业评估和传播可信度的有条件、近端推断，而非持久的声誉、品牌资产或市场绩效主张。

Conclusion: 结论定义了"负责任的透明"作为建议的应对配置，结合及时通知、可理解的说明、角色责任确认、补救措施、纠正证据和追索机制。该理论调和了既有文献中关于AI相关责任归因的矛盾发现。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly enact market-facing promises through chatbots, recommendation systems, automated decisions, and generative interfaces. Their failures, misuse, and misrepresentation raise a question that conventional brand-crisis models do not fully specify: how do stakeholders assign responsibility when technical causation, customer-facing control, and governance duties are distributed across an AI system, developer, deployer, vendor, and user? This conceptual paper develops a sociotechnical process theory from a structured, federated scoping synthesis of verified academic and primary sources. It distinguishes an AI/algorithmic incident from an AI-related organisational crisis and, in turn, from an AI-related organisational scandal. The framework proposes that incident configuration shapes actor-specific attribution; attribution informs capability, integrity, fairness, and relationship appraisals; and public moralisation may, but need not, escalate an incident into scandal. The theory offers a reconciliation of findings that algorithm involvement can buffer negative brand reactions in some settings while robot and chatbot failures can redirect responsibility to an associated firm in others. It introduces accountable transparency as a proposed response configuration that combines timely notice, an intelligible account, role-responsibility acknowledgement, remedy, evidence of correction, and recourse. The evidence supports conditional, proximal inferences about blame, trust, satisfaction, firm evaluation, and communication credibility more strongly than claims about durable reputation, brand equity, or market performance.

</details>


### [293] [ISO-RAG: Isoperimetric Noise Control for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00513)
*Siyuan Zhang,Hanchen Wang,Dong Wen,Ying Zhang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ISO-RAG，一种基于几何感知的检索增强生成框架。通过将知识图谱投影到双曲庞加莱球中预计算节点的等周轮廓，在检索时剪除虚假边并将搜索空间限制在局部子图，从而调控Personalized PageRank扩散过程，解决图基RAG在复杂多跳问答中的语义漂移和高延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稠密检索难以处理多跳问答中复杂的推理路径，而图基RAG虽然能捕获多步关系，但在全局图遍历时面临严重的语义漂移和在线高延迟问题。因此需要一种既能利用图结构多跳信息又能避免全局遍历噪声的方法。

Method: 将知识图谱嵌入双曲庞加莱球的几何空间中，预计算节点级的等周轮廓（几何不变量）；在检索阶段利用这些轮廓剪除虚假边，将搜索空间严格限制在局部子图中；通过这种拓扑纯化来调控PPR扩散过程，实现精确且低延迟的收敛。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试中，ISO-RAG相比SOTA基线在检索召回率上平均绝对提升10.0%，在下游精确匹配（EM）上平均绝对提升4.3%，实现了更优的精度-效率权衡，从根本上消除了全局遍历带来的延迟瓶颈。

Conclusion: ISO-RAG通过几何感知的图剪枝策略，将问题限定在局部子图进行检索，既保留了图结构推理优势又大幅降低了延迟，验证了拓扑方法在RAG系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates large language models (LLMs) hallucinations, yet conventional dense retrieval struggles with the complex reasoning paths of multi-hop question answering (QA). Graph-based RAG captures multi-step relationships but suffers from severe semantic drift and high online latency due to noisy global graph traversals. Thus, we propose ISO-RAG (ISOperimetric Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a geometry-aware RAG framework. By projecting the underlying knowledge graph into a hyperbolic Poincare ball to precompute node-wise isoperimetric profiles, ISO-RAG prunes spurious edges during retrieval, restricting the search space to a strictly localized subgraph. This topological purification regulates Personalized PageRank (PPR) diffusion driving the retrieval process, ensuring exact and low-latency convergence. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ISO-RAG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by average absolute gains of 10.0% in retrieval recall and 4.3% in downstream exact match, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off by fundamentally eliminating the latency bottleneck of global traversals. Our source code is available at https://github.com/ZaiizaiZHANG/ISO-RAG.

</details>


### [294] [Feedback-Assisted Trust Propagation over Document Relation Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00543)
*Zhuoheng Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TrustPropRAG，通过构建文档关系图并进行多跳传播来估计文档可靠性信任分数，利用有限的用户反馈信号扩展到整个语料库，从而提升RAG系统的检索质量和答案可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖的外部语料库可能包含过时、矛盾、嘈杂或不可靠的文档，引入可靠性风险。已有工作利用文档关系改进答案可靠性，但可靠性信号难以在直接比较的文档对之外传播，且人工反馈收集成本高昂。

Method: TrustPropRAG将文档关系结构化为图，基于图进行多跳传播估计文档可靠性。它采用有限的人类反馈作为锚点，通过构建优化问题联合刻画成对文档关系与用户反馈，为每篇文档计算信任分数，利用这些分数改善可靠文档的选择并支持信任感知的答案生成。

Result: 实验评估显示，TrustPropRAG在检索质量和精确匹配（exact match）方面均优于基线方法，并且在稀疏和带噪声的反馈条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TrustPropRAG通过图上的多跳信任传播，有效弥合了昂贵的用户反馈与大规模语料库之间的鸿沟，增强了RAG系统的整体可靠性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems rely on external corpora that may contain outdated, contradictory, noisy, or unreliable documents, introducing reliability risks. Prior work has leveraged document relations to improve the answer reliability of RAG. To propagate reliability signals beyond directly compared document pairs, we propose TrustPropRAG, which structures document relations as a graph and estimates document reliability through multi-hop propagation across the graph. TrustPropRAG anchors this propagation with a limited set of human feedback on document reliability, extending these costly-to-collect feedback-based reliability signals across the whole corpus. Specifically, based on the constructed document relation graph, TrustPropRAG estimates a trust score for each document by formulating and solving an optimization problem that jointly captures pairwise document relations and user feedback. These scores are then used to improve the selection of reliable documents and support trust-aware answer generation. Evaluation results show that TrustPropRAG improves both retrieval quality and exact match over baselines, and remains robust under sparse and noisy feedback.

</details>


### [295] [VoiceLongMemEval: Do Assistants Remember How You Sounded?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00570)
*Ramit Pahwa,Parivesh Priye,Apoorva Beedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VoiceLongMemEval (VLME)基准，用于评估大型语言模型在长程多会话对话历史中利用副语言元数据（情感标签、韵律描述、语音事件）进行推理的能力。实验发现前沿模型存在普遍的"情感鸿沟"：仅提供文本副语言元数据即可带来0.09-0.38的准确率提升，而标准ASR流水线会系统性丢弃这些信号，音频原生模型则能直接提取这些线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话历史基准仅将其视为信息检索、长时推理或知识更新任务，完全忽略了人机交互的基本动态——即"说话方式"这一副语言维度。该信号在标准ASR转写中不可恢复，亟需专门基准来揭示并评估这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建VLME基准：(1) 每个问答答案依赖附加在对话轮次上的副语言元数据（情感标签、韵律描述符、语音事件）；(2) 所有条目经过三阶段对抗性门控，确保仅凭文本转录时强语言模型无法作答；(3) 系统评估前沿模型和有权重模型在此类任务上的表现。

Result: 实验揭示普遍存在"情感鸿沟"：提供文本轨副语言元数据带来0.09到0.38的准确率提升（提供证据提示时达0.61到0.69）；标准ASR流水线系统性地丢弃该信号；音频原生模型可直接从语音中提取线索（0.354-0.412 vs 盲基线0.325）。

Conclusion: 现有主流LLM在利用副语言信息进行长程对话推理方面存在系统性不足，音频原生模型展现出提取这些线索的潜力，为未来多模态对话智能体的设计提供了重要方向。

Abstract: With the growing scale of multi-agent architectures and large language models, deployed AI assistants are increasingly tasked with reasoning over long, continuous, multi-session conversation histories. Current benchmarks evaluate this dialogue history as information retrieval over long horizon, temporal reasoning, or knowledge updates, while crucially ignoring the fundamental dynamics of human-agent interaction, i.e. how they said it. To address this gap, we present VoiceLongMemEval (VLME) benchmark, where every answer depends on paralinguistic metadata (emotion labels, prosody descriptors, and voice events) attached to conversational turns, which is otherwise unrecoverable from the words alone. Every item passes a three-stage adversarial gate, ensuring that a strong language model fails when given only the transcript. Evaluating leading frontier and open-weight models reveals a pervasive affect gap; providing text-track paralinguistic metadata yields a 0.09 to 0.38 accuracy boost (0.61 to 0.69 when prompted with evidence hints), while standard ASR pipelines systematically discard this signal. Additionally, audio-native models successfully extract these cues directly from speech (0.354 to 0.412 vs. 0.325 blind). Code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [296] [Residual Sparsification via Output Importance for Compressing Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00575)
*Seungwoo Jung,Dohyeok Kwon,Seungmin Cha,Junseok Lee,Yeonho Yoo,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSER是一种新的MoE模型残差稀疏化压缩方法，将压缩目标从"最小化单个矩阵误差"转变为"保留专家输出精度"，通过引入输出重要性度量来解决现有方法目标不一致的问题，在Qwen和DeepSeek上分别将压缩前后精度差距缩小1.41倍和1.44倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构高效扩展大语言模型但GPU内存需求巨大，常需压缩。残差稀疏化将专家投影矩阵分解为共享基矩阵和逐专家残差矩阵后压缩残差。现有方法独立最小化每个残差矩阵的压缩误差，但论文分析表明此目标与压缩后保持模型精度不一致——因为在专家内部，最终输出由多个投影和隐状态耦合计算产生，单个矩阵的小误差会通过隐状态传播和投影交互被放大，导致专家输出误差增大和精度下降。

Method: PARSER提出将压缩目标从最小化孤立矩阵误差转变为保留专家输出误差。核心创新是引入"输出重要性"（output importance）度量，衡量每个残差矩阵分量对专家输出误差的实际贡献，据此指导压缩决策。

Result: 实验表明，相比现有方法，PARSER在Qwen上将压缩模型与未压缩模型的精度差距缩小1.41倍，在DeepSeek上缩小1.44倍，同时实现了相同的峰值内存缩减。

Conclusion: PARSER验证了MoE压缩中目标对齐的重要性：从输出精度而非孤立矩阵误差角度进行残差稀疏化，可以在不牺牲压缩效率（同等内存节省）的情况下显著提升压缩后的模型精度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models efficiently, but they demand massive GPU memory. To cope with such demand, models are commonly compressed to reduce their memory footprint. Residual sparsification is a representative compression technique that decomposes each projection matrix of an expert into a shared base matrix and per-expert residual matrix, and then compresses the residuals. Existing sparsification methods compress each residual matrix independently by minimizing its compression error, thereby minimizing the error of each projection matrix. However, our analysis shows that this objective is misaligned with preserving model accuracy after compression. In an expert, the final output is produced through computations coupled across multiple projections and hidden representations. Therefore, even small errors in individual matrices can propagate through hidden representations and projection interactions, leading to large expert output errors and accuracy degradation. To address this misalignment, we propose PARSER, a new residual sparsification method that shifts the compression objective from minimizing isolated matrix errors to preserving the expert output error. PARSER achieves this by introducing output importance, which measures the actual contribution to the expert output error. Our experiments show that, compared with existing methods, PARSER narrows the accuracy gap to the uncompressed model by 1.41$\times$ on Qwen and 1.44$\times$ on DeepSeek, while achieving the same peak memory reduction.

</details>


### [297] [Consistency Without Alignment: Item-Sensitive Language Models Indistinguishable From Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00576)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》抽象出的二选一信号任务，证明item-sensitivity（对输入内容敏感而非依赖输出先验）虽是任务胜任力的必要证据但非充分条件。实验覆盖7个语言模型、2个模型家族、后训练消融和3种评分规则，21个模型×规则组合全部可靠地表现出item-sensitivity，但8/21与随机选择无统计差异，5个在描述目标上差于随机。item-sensitivity与随机距离相关性仅r=0.30，作者称之为"无对齐的一致性"，并证明其泛化至任何依赖item-sensitivity、排列一致性或自一致性但缺乏独立参照的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估中普遍将item-sensitivity作为任务胜任力的证据，但缺乏严格检验该证据是否充分。作者在可计算封闭形式参照点（适应最大化、后验最大化、均匀随机）的环境中，探究item-sensitivity是否足以说明模型真正理解任务而非进行表面匹配。

Method: 从桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》抽象出被迫选择信号任务，环境中三种参照策略（fit-maximising、posterior-maximising、uniform random）均可闭式计算。测试7个LM、2个模型家族、一个后训练消融及3个独立评分规则，共21个模型×规则单元，系统性比较item-sensitivity、与随机的统计距离及描述目标的准确性。

Result: 21个模型×规则单元全部可靠地表现出item-sensitivity，但其中8个与忽略输入的随机选择无统计差异，5个在目标描述上低于随机。item-sensitivity与随机距离相关性仅r=0.30。此外，无语用学的字面相似度基线优于多数LLM；在两层基线相似度源上叠加语用层使chooser更趋随机而非趋近贝叶斯参照；标准多选题格式在此无显著内容信号。

Conclusion: item-sensitivity是任务胜任力的必要但非充分证据。评估若仅依赖item-sensitivity、排列一致性或自一致性而无独立参照，将面临"无对齐的一致性"风险。该发现泛化性强，提示LLM评估需要更严格的独立参照基准。

Abstract: Item-sensitivity, defined as whether a model's choice depends on the specific input rather than on its own output prior, is widely reported as evidence of task competence. We show this evidence is necessary but not sufficient using a forced-choice signalling task abstracted from the board game Deception: Murder in Hong Kong. In this environment, the reference points against which a coordinate should be judged (a fit-maximising strategy, a posterior-maximising strategy, and uniform random selection) are all computable in closed form. Across seven language models, two model families, a post-training ablation, and three independent scoring rules, every one of 21 model-by-rule cells is reliably item-sensitive. Yet 8 of those 21 cells are not statistically distinguishable from a chooser that ignores the item and selects at random, and 5 score worse than random at describing the target. Item-sensitivity and distance from random correlate at only r = 0.30. We call this consistency without alignment and argue it generalises to any evaluation that relies on item-sensitivity, permutation consistency, or self-consistency without an independent reference for the measured quantity. We further find that a literal-similarity baseline with no pragmatics outperforms most tested language models, that adding a pragmatic layer over two baseline similarity sources moves choosers toward random rather than toward the Bayesian reference, and that a standard labelled multiple-choice format carries no measurable content signal here. All results represent the model side of a pre-registered instrument; a matched human condition is designed and piloted but not yet collected.

</details>


### [298] [Same Request, Different Boundary: Evaluating Cybersecurity Assistance across Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00578)
*Rui Yang,Yang Hong,Yichao Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出3R-Bench基准，包含150个真实网络安全请求及其两种对抗性对话设置，评估8个LLM后发现：先前的对话历史（尤其是已接受的请求）会显著改变模型对相同请求的回应——合规率从被拒绝历史后的62.0%升至被接受历史后的85.1%；对话分解则使合规率从直接请求的501/800降至172/800。现有网络安全数据集均未考虑对话上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在网络安全等高危领域可能被滥用，模型提供商需要区分恶意用户和合法防御者。现有网络安全特定数据集评估了拒绝机制，但没有考虑请求的对话上下文，这会导致对安全审查机制的评估不全面且不准确。

Method: 构建3R-Bench（Refusal, Repetition, and Revision）基准：包含150个真实网络安全请求，并为其配备两种对抗性对话设置（对话历史操控与对话分解），在8个LLM上评估其安全拒绝行为的一致性。

Result: 先前的助手行为会强烈改变对未变请求的响应：376个可用配对中，合规率从被拒绝历史后的62.0%上升到被接受历史后的85.1%。对话分解下则呈现相反模式：合规率从直接请求的501/800降至对话后的172/800；在738个两个条件下均返回模型生成文本的配对中，降幅达45.1个百分点。失败反馈仅能恢复该损失的一小部分。

Conclusion: LLM的安全拒绝机制在对话上下文中存在显著不一致性——对话历史可以提升或大幅压制模型对相同危险请求的合规率，表明现有的单轮安全评测不足以反映真实对话场景下的安全风险，3R-Bench可作为该方向评测的补充工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can solve complex problems, but their misuse in high-risk domains can lead to severe consequences. Model providers therefore restrict assistance for potentially harmful requests. Refusing all cybersecurity requests would therefore harm legitimate users. Providers need a mechanism to block malicious use without denying legitimate assistance to defenders. Existing cybersecurity-specific datasets evaluate this mechanism, but none considers the conversational context of a request. We introduce 3R-Bench (Refusal, Repetition, and Revision), a benchmark of 150 real-world cybersecurity requests augmented with two adversarial conversational settings, and evaluate eight LLMs on it. Prior assistant behavior strongly changes responses to an unchanged request: among 376 available pairs from a 400-pair panel, compliance rises from 62.0% after refused history to 85.1% after accepted history. The opposite pattern appears under dialogue decomposition. In comparison, compliance falls from 501/800 direct responses to 172/800 after dialogue; among 738 pairs returning model-authored text in both conditions, the decrease is 45.1 points. Failure feedback recovers only a small fraction of this loss.

</details>


### [299] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三种人机交互学习设计（不受限聊天机器人、苏格拉底式受限机器人、基于脑电信号自适应调整难度的非对话式辅导系统），发现不受限的AI聊天机器人在即时后测中学习增益最高（p<.03, d>0.80），而自适应条件产生更高的EEG认知投入（p=.018）。然而，聚类分析表明不受限模式的用户主要采用直接获取答案的策略，其"成功"更可能是即时评测的结果而非深层学习的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨不受限的AI访问是否绕过了学习所需的认知努力，还是只是简化了知识获取过程。通过对比不同交互设计，理解AI在不同约束方式下对人类学习的实际影响。

Method: 50名参与者学习核安全协议（选择该领域因参与者零先验知识基线）。实验流程包括：教学视频→前测→三种AI交互条件下的评估阶段→即时后测。使用Muse头带测量所有条件下的认知投入。三种条件为：(1) 不受限对话机器人（类ChatGPT）；(2) 苏格拉底模式（引导性提示但不给最终答案）；(3) 非对话式自适应辅导系统（基于脑电认知投入实时调节难度）。

Result: 不受限聊天机器人的学习增益显著高于两种受约束模式（p<.03, d>0.80）；自适应条件产生显著更高的EEG认知投入（p=.018）。聚类分析显示，不受限模式下大多数用户采用直接答案检索策略；苏格拉底模式用户最初尝试推理但逐渐脱离参与。

Conclusion: 不受限AI的高学习增益并非深层学习的证据，而是即时时后测直接反映了训练阶段的答案检索行为。这提示需要重新审视AI辅助学习的评估方式，区分"即时知识获取"与"深层学习"。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>


### [300] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体LLM系统中提示优化时两类角色——内容生成与执行协议——纠缠的问题，并提出"控制-数据流分离"框架：将执行关键控制表示为类型化、可验证的程序对象，而任务相关语言保持为可优化的数据流。在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级工作流中，该框架实现了100%的协议最终有效性并持续提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的提示优化存在根本性矛盾：被优化的提示往往同时承担两个纠缠的角色——生成任务相关内容，以及指定执行关键协议（如消息路由、输出格式、终止信号），而底层代码依赖这些协议。内容生成的提示编辑可能无意破坏协议，导致整个智能体管道失败。现有方法未能区分这两类不同性质的提示内容。

Method: 关键观察是两类角色具有不同的表征形式：执行协议通常是结构化的，而任务相关内容通常以非结构化语言表达。基于此提出控制-数据流分离框架：将执行关键控制建模为类型化、经过验证的程序对象，任务相关语言则作为可优化的数据流用于智能体间通信。该设计使优化器能改进多智能体行为，而不将路由或格式化接口暴露给提示漂移风险。

Result: 在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级三个工作流中，框架经验性地实现了100%的协议最终有效性（即协议始终得以保持），同时一致性地提升了任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过区分结构化协议与自由文本内容，并将协议从可优化提示中分离出来，可以在提升多智能体行为表现的同时消除提示优化对协议完整性的威胁，实现安全且有效的提示优化。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [301] [REVISE: Validity-Guided Recovery for Online Revisions in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00643)
*Ruoling Qi,Xuaner Wu,Penghang Liu,Jian Chen,Yirui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Revise，一个基于有效性引导的运行时系统，用于在结构化智能体工作流中实现细粒度的恢复。当修订到达时，Revise通过将增量与数据/控制依赖相交、将影响传播到部分执行的DAG来确定受影响的工作，仅停止无效工作并重算受影响区域，保留已确立的有效进度。实验表明其在300个挑战性修订/提交执行中与最新版本oracle匹配，无陈旧输出或效应，相较完整重启减少40.6–56.0%的模型调用，相较后缀重算减少31.3–43.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体修订过程中存在正确性与效率的根本权衡：丢弃进行中的工作可保证最新版本正确性但浪费仍有效的进度，而重用先前工作可保持效率但有传播陈旧状态到输出和工具效应的风险。现有恢复策略以粗粒度策略不均衡地解决此权衡，要么允许可能陈旧的工作继续（偏效率），要么从最早冲突处重启或重算线性后缀（偏正确性），丢弃未受影响的进度。

Method: Revise采用有效性引导的细粒度恢复机制：当修订到达时，(1) 将修订增量与记录的数据依赖和控制依赖求交；(2) 将影响传播通过部分执行的DAG以识别受影响的工作；(3) 停止无效工作、保留最早冲突之后已确立有效性的进度；(4) 仅重算受影响区域。不完整的溯源会保守地扩展恢复范围，重用的结果在提交前会被重新验证。

Result: 真实编码智能体轨迹分析显示在线恢复机会显著：118个会话在排队消息投递前保留了可观察工作，167个重叠assistant响应中入队到完成的重叠在p95达到56.55秒。在300个挑战性修订/提交执行中，Revise与最新版本oracle匹配且无陈旧输出或效应。在未修改的LangGraph和LLMCompiler应用（Qwen3-14B）上，相较完整重启减少40.6–56.0%模型调用，相较后缀重算减少31.3–43.6%；在服务压力下，修订到正确完成token减少13.26%，SLO goodput提升3.07–5.43%。

Conclusion: Revise通过细粒度的有效性引导恢复机制有效化解了智能体修订中正确性与效率的冲突，在保持最新版本正确性（无陈旧输出或效应）的同时大幅减少不必要的重算开销，为结构化智能体工作流的并发修订恢复提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Agent revisions expose a fundamental correctness--efficiency trade-off during concurrent execution. Discarding ongoing work preserves latest-version correctness but wastes progress that may remain valid, whereas reusing prior work preserves efficiency but risks propagating stale state into outputs and tool effects. Existing recovery strategies resolve this trade-off in an imbalanced way with coarse-grained policies: they either favor efficiency by allowing potentially stale work to continue, or favor correctness by restarting the workflow or recomputing a linear suffix from the earliest conflict, thereby discarding unaffected progress. We present \textsc{Revise}, a validity-guided runtime for fine-grained recovery in structured agent workflows. When a revision arrives, \textsc{Revise} first intersects its delta with recorded data and control dependencies and propagates the resulting impact through the partially executed DAG to identify affected work. It then stops invalid work, preserves validity-established progress beyond the earliest conflict, and recomputes only the affected region. Incomplete provenance conservatively expands recovery, while reused results are revalidated before commit. Analysis of real coding-agent traces show online recovery opportunities: 118 sessions retain observable work before a queued later message is delivered; across 167 overlapping assistant responses, enqueue-to-completion overlap reaches 56.55~s at p95. Across 300 challenging revision/commit executions, \textsc{Revise} matches a latest-version oracle with no stale outputs or effects. On unmodified LangGraph and LLMCompiler applications using Qwen3-14B, it reduces model calls by 40.6--56.0\% relative to full restart and by 31.3--43.6\% relative to suffix recomputation. Under serving pressure, it further reduces revision-to-correct-completion tokens by 13.26\% and improves SLO goodput by 3.07--5.43\%.

</details>


### [302] [DramaChain Bench: An End-to-End Benchmark for Short-Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00646)
*Haoyuan Shi,Mingtao Chen,Shuo Jiang,Ziyan Chen,Xuyi Sheng,Yiming Liu,Ying Zhang,Miao Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Fanyang Lu,Songyuanyi Lu,Xiele Wu,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Richeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DramaChain Bench——首个覆盖短剧完整制作链（剧本、分镜、关键帧、镜头级视频到成片）的基准。它基于统一维度系统（5条评估轴、63个叶子维度），由人类标注系统生成17,488个有效分数和255,925条可追溯归因记录，并配有Agentic Judge自动评分器，在0.918的PLCC下重现模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短剧基准仅评估视频生成阶段，且使用预先撰写的输入而非真实上游管线输出，导致两个关键问题无法回答：各阶段是否忠于原始剧本意图（而非仅忠于直接输入提示），以及多镜头在组装为多集成片后是否保持连贯性。需要覆盖全流程的基准来验证上游缺陷是否级联影响最终成片质量。

Method: 提出三个协同系统：1）DramaChain Dimensions——在每一阶段实例化的五条评估轴，细化为63个叶子维度；2）DramaChain Agent校准自商业短剧平台的工作流与成片质量，支持跨模型逐阶段公平比较；3）DramaChain Labeling System——每项由三位专业标注员独立打分，缺陷按时空定位并选自预定义缺陷清单。DramaChain Agentic Judge通过多轮智能体证据收集后依据逐项目检查表自动评分所有叶子维度。

Result: 数据集包含5,785个条目，产生17,488个有效分数和255,925条可追溯归因记录。人工标注证明上游缺陷沿管线级联，说明最终剧集质量并非仅由视频生成决定。DramaChain Agentic Judge以平均PLCC 0.918重现模型排名，足以在零标注成本下准入新模型。

Conclusion: DramaChain Bench填补了短剧全制作链评估的空白，实证表明成片质量受上游各阶段缺陷级联影响而非仅受视频生成制约；其自动评判器具备高相关性，可免标注成本地扩展模型评测，为该领域提供了首个端到端可归因的标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Commercial short-drama production follows a multi-stage chain: script, storyboard, keyframe imagery, shot-level video, and the finished short drama. Most existing benchmarks evaluate solely the video-generation stage using pre-authored inputs instead of real upstream pipeline outputs. This leaves two critical questions unanswerable: whether each stage adheres to the original script intent (rather than only its immediate input prompt), and whether disparate shots remain coherent after assembly into multi-episode releases. We present DramaChain Bench, the first short-drama benchmark that evaluates every stage of the complete production chain. It is built upon three in-house systems sharing one dimension system, DramaChain Dimensions: five evaluation axes instantiated at every stage, resolving into 63 leaf dimensions. DramaChain Agent is calibrated against commercial short-drama platforms in both workflow and finished short-drama quality, enabling stage-wise fair comparison across models. DramaChain Labeling System has each of the 5,785 items scored independently by three professional annotators, with all defects spatio-temporally localised and selected from a predefined defect list. This process produces 17,488 valid scores and 255,925 traceable attribution records. The human annotations confirm that upstream defects cascade across the pipeline, demonstrating that final episode quality is not governed by video generation alone. DramaChain Agentic Judge then scores every leaf dimension automatically, gathering evidence over multiple agentic rounds before judging against a per-item checklist; it reproduces the model ranking at a mean PLCC of 0.918, enough to admit new models at no annotation cost.

</details>


### [303] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"环境接地审计"方法，在Contexto进化搜索任务中，对语言模型智能体的自报告（置信度、理由、适应性报告）进行精确、无人工标注的结果验证。通过200次运行、四种报告配置、12,249份自报告，检验三个假设均失败：置信度校准失败（高估成功率4.8-9.3倍）、继承理由无益于后续提议、适应性选择无法改善报告质量。结论是应将智能体自报告视为待验证的主张，而非可靠性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体的自报告（置信度和理由）被当作便捷的监控信号，但"便捷不等于验证"。现有监控方式缺乏对智能体中间每一步提议的精确、客观结果核验，作者希望建立一种可以精确量化的审计方法，检验自报告是否真实可靠。

Method: 设计一个环境接地审计框架：让语言模型操作进化式Contexto搜索，其反馈函数为每个有效猜测分配一个无需人类标注的精确排名。在五种配置、三个模型家族上进行200次运行，其中四种报告配置产生12,249份自报告。通过受控干预（对754个继承理由进行扰动）、适应性选择与随机选择的对比实验，分别检验三个假设：①陈述置信度是否校准；②继承理由是否影响后续提议；③适应性选择是否能提升报告质量。

Result: 三个假设全部失败：①操作者将前100名成功率高估了4.8至9.3倍，校准与判别能力在不同模型家族中发生分离；②对754个继承理由的受控干预表明，真实理由带来的任何可测量收益上限约为250个排名；③适应性与随机选择均未产生显著的选择差异或亲子代之间的报告准确率传递，尽管两者的搜索行为截然不同。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体的自报告不应被视为其自身可靠性的证据，而应被当作需要通过环境验证的主张（claims）。监控信号只能提供便捷性，必须通过环境接地的方式加以核实，否则无法保证置信度、理由或基于适应度的报告的真实价值。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


### [304] [SciTrue: Reliable Scientific Claim Validation with Frontier and Open Language Models at the NTCIR SciClaimEval Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00654)
*Qiming Bao,Neşet Özkan Tan,Siyuan Wang,Mark Gahegan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SciTrue团队在NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval任务两个子任务中的参与方案，采用十一款前沿多模态模型的诚实基准测试配合轻量后处理，在四个证据类别/子任务组合中的三个获得第一名，并在第四个组合的首要指标上并列第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明验证任务需要基于论文的表格和图表验证声明，作者希望通过系统性地对比多种前沿与开源多模态模型，而非单一模型调优，来评估当前模型在该任务上的真实能力，并揭示任务结构本身带来的优化空间。

Method: 采用十一款（包括Claude Opus 4.8、Gemma-4-31B、GPT-5.5、Claude Fable 5等）前沿和开源多模态模型，在统一的逐样本诚实协议下进行基准评测，配合轻量透明的后处理。关键方法包括：利用任务配对结构设计无泄漏的配对先验（从声明文本本身恢复Supported/Refuted配对），将高置信度证据分配给Supported标签；还包括受控微调、蒸馏和智能体一致性检查。

Result: 在官方盲测排行榜的三个证据类别/子任务组合中获得第一名，第四个组合的首要指标上并列第一。Subtask-2达到97.7的分数。配对先验技术将Subtask-1配对准确率从72.2提升至93.5，远超过任何模型替换或集成加权带来的提升。

Conclusion: 强指令微调模型已具备竞争力，任务配对结构是最大的性能杠杆，大多数残余错误来自视觉上无法检测的标签映射交换或数据集标签噪声。此外作者记录了测量泄漏问题——数据打包的文件排序编码了标签信息，导致标签信息绕过数据内容本身到达系统。

Abstract: We describe the SciTrue team's participation in both subtasks of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval task~\cite{sciclaimeval}, which asks systems to verify scientific claims against the tables and figures of a paper. Rather than tuning a single model, we benchmark eleven frontier and open multimodal models under one honest, per-sample protocol and combine them with light, transparent post-processing. On the official, blind test leaderboard (Section~\ref{sec:results}), SciTrue placed first by a clear margin in three of the four evidence-category/subtask combinations, and tied for first on the primary metric in the fourth. Three findings explain the result.
